Public Information of the General Secretariat of Defence/DNA is part of the Public Service The Press Office, divided into five Sections and a Secretariat, carries out various tasks. First of all, it promptly informs the Minister and the Head of Cabinet on the news reported by national and foreign media outlets; prepares the daily press review; processes and coordinates news of military interest for the "Televideo" and "Intemet" service, regulating the methods and times of publication; prepares and disseminates press releases, news and statements regarding the Minister; ensures information action relating to the Minister's activities in the national, intemational and NATO fields; directs and coordinates, according to the Minister's directives, the Public Information activity of the Ministry, also making use of the central and peripheral Public Information Bodies of the Armed Forces.
The components of the Armed Forces depend on the UPI and therefore on the Chief of Defense Staff: the Public Information Agency (API) for the Army, the Office for Extemal Relations Documentation of Promotional Activities (REDAP) for the Navy, the Office Public Information for the Air Force and the Public Information Office for the Carabinieri.
The Head of the Press Office of the Minister's Cabinet is the Head of the Public Information Service of the Ministry of Defense and, as such, issues provisions in the public information sector and guides the public information activity of the central and peripheral bodies.
With the new structure of the Cabinet of the Minister of Defence, the role of an office in direct collaboration with the Minister himself as responsible for defense communication policy.
Public Information of the Armed Forces reports to the Public Information Office of the Defense General Staff (UPI) which, among other things, takes care of operational public information and produces the magazine "Information of the Defense".
The Peripheral Bodies (Press Officers of High Commands and other Bodies), on the other hand, deal with news published by the local press of political and/or military interest at a national level, and/or which strictly concem the technical-military activity of the Commands and Peripheral Bodies. ____________ The Central Bodies deal with news regarding the activities of the Chiefs of Staff, the General Secretary/DNA and the Armed Forces as a whole; disseminate press releases, denials or clarifications, subject to authorization from the Minister; they participate in exhibition activities at a national and intemational level and collaborate with bodies, institutes and organizations for the development of statistical, historical, sociological and scientific studies conceming the Armed Forces.

In France, the Ministry of Defense has given Public Information a leading role by establishing, on 27 July 1998, the DICoD (Delegation for the Information and Communication of the Defense department) with the specific task of strengthening the link between public opinion and the Armed Forces, to promote the image of the latter, to coordinate and disseminate information and communications relating In Germany, IP is delegated to PAOs (Public Affairs Officers, there are 28 throughout the country) who are the territorial spokespersons (Military Districts, HQs, Military Regions and Schools). To be designated PAO, Officers must have full command of the English language and have attended a specific course consisting of six weeks at the Academy for Information and Communication (AIK) + 4 weeks of practice in newspapers, TV and radio.
The structure of the Implementing Regulation, approved with Presidential Decree no. 422 of 21 September 2001, is therefore divided into two directions: the regime when fully operational (24 months after the entry into force of the regulation), which concems the qualifications that will be necessary to staff to be used for information and communication activities; the transitional discipline, which concems the training activities prescribed for those who, despite already carrying out institutional communication activities, and therefore having a certain experience in the sector, do not however possess the cognitive tools, adequately updated, to carry out their duties with the development of new communication techniques.
The law aims to ensure that the staff assigned to the press offices and public relations offices acquire a high level of professionalism while allowing the Public Administration to have, intemally, the human resources adequate for communication tasks.
For completeness, it seems appropriate to mention the different level of preparation of PI personnel in two European countries.
The approval of Law 150/2000 and the issuing of the consequent Implementing Regulation therefore entail the need for a timely adaptation of the Public Information structures of the Ministry of Defence. Currently, in fact, apart from a few exceptions, in the Defense press offices only a very limited number of Officers possess the requirements established by law and the personnel to be employed in the press offices are taken from other functions/specialisations and "transferred" without receiving any prior training . The problem of the lack of training also concems the figures of the directors responsible for the Armed Forces magazines. The anomaly was filled, in agreement with the National Order of Joumalists, by resorting to registration in a Special List of Responsible Editors not registered in the register of the Order itself which, however, has repeatedly recommended the regularization of the position .
The law, also having to address the regulation of the requirements that public personnel must possess in order to carry out information and communication functions, has delegated this task to a regulatory source. The art. 5 has in fact provided for the adoption of a regulation to identify the qualifications for access by personnel to be used for information and communication activities and to regulate training interventions for those personnel who already carry out the said activities. preparation and communication of information to citizens, both by identifying forms, tools, communication products and training methods, and by regulating the structures of the offices responsible for communication activities.
Finally, the joumalistic specificity of the job of press officer presupposes knowledge of the world of information, especially that of the market. And this knowledge is not only based on the daily vision of agencies and newspapers or television news, but also requires the ability to perceive the trend lines of joumalism, i.e. the editorial policies that are at the origin of the "information product". ____________

The political openness of the former USSR allowed for a more frank dialogue with those govemment authorities, which materialized with the first contacts suitable for agreeing on the methods for identifying war burials and the subsequent repatriation of the bodies of the fallen.

12.5.4 State property issues The legislation on the Fallen has essentially remained the same as that of the 1930s. The precepts contained therein have gradually lost their effectiveness as the question of "honoring the fallen" has gradually transformed into the need to maintain burial structures. In this context, a revision of the regulatory framework appears necessary, to allow the full implementation of the law of 9 January 1951 (art. 7, paragraph 1) conceming the taking over, by the Municipalities, of the war burial grounds existing in the territory of their respective competence. ____________ 12.5.5 MISCELLANEOUS 12.5.5.1 Relations with the Associations Following the intensification of the operational activity carried out by ONORCADUTI in the years 1989-1997, relations with the various Armed Forces and Combatant Associations further increased, in particular with the National Union Intemees and Veterans of Russia (UNIRR) which provided valid support to the solution of the problems regarding the fate of Italian soldiers fallen and missing in Russia.

Possible evolutionary lines for the Army The land instrument with a mixed volunteer-lever composition, foreseen by Legislative Decree 27 June 2000 n. 214 for a total of 137,000 men, has therefore undergone a significant modification for the approval of Law 14 November 2000, n. 331 on the professional military service which reduced its size to 112,000 units to be achieved by 2004.
This restructuring requires an impressive commitment not only from a quantitative point of view (a further overall reduction equal to approximately 20% compared to the structure outlined in the model mixed), but also for the cultural leap that it implies and for the speed with which it is necessary to proceed. Add to this the burden that the Armed Force has been supporting, uninterruptedly for about twenty years (the first mission can be considered that of Beirut in 1982), outside the national territory.
The current situation therefore aims to provide the basic elements for identifying trend lines aimed at seeking the most appropriate solutions to the needs of national security within the framework of a military policy increasingly consistent with the principles of management economics.
Below are indicated what are considered possible evolutionary lines to be studied in depth as topics of study by specific working groups.
The transition phase is still underway, as there have been and remain many repercussions on personnel, resources, the efficiency of the system and on the relationship between financial resources and tasks to be carried out, especially in consideration of the changed geo-strategic scenario of this part of the year 2001. ____________ Possible evolutionary lines of restructuring Part II illustrated the legislative provisions adopted from December 1995 to May 2001, following which the defense structure changed and is still changing its architecture.

There are many shortcomings in operational capacity, so it will be necessary to concentrate and rationalize the central bodies and the command and support structures of the forces, both intemally and intemationally, in order to recover personnel, resources and infrastructures for the benefit of the strengthening of operational forces. These constitute, in fact, the primary instrument of military policy which, since 11 September, has found itself and will find itself facing a whole set of situations and tasks characterized by contemporaneity and also by different spatial locations. are achievable with the “simple reductive restructuring in progress”.
This means equipping ourselves with extremely flexible structures, capable of dealing with diversified situations which can also present themselves as emergencies and therefore require the maximum timeliness of reaction. Hence the opportunity to further deepen the current command organisation, both to be able to identify and project commands at different levels with its own organic units, and to automatically deal with the tumover needs of the commands and units employed abroad.

Possible evolutionary lines for the Carabinieri The processes of simplification and computerization of procedures and those of reorganization of all logistical and administrative activities have allowed and will continue to allow for significant recoveries of strength, all aimed at strengthening operational activities.
In this direction it is possible to achieve management savings, in line with the economic principle of centralization in case of shortage of resources and also in compliance with the military criterion of closely combining the training and employment phases. In this case, one of the topics to be explored further must be that of the convenience of merging the Air Squadron Command with the Air Force Command, as well as the personnel sector and some logistics bodies in correlation with the similar structures of the other Armed Forces or the General Directorates.
Also for the Navy, as for the Army, it is necessary to deepen the overall structure to achieve the best possible relationship between the command and support component and the operational naval component.
To ensure the full operation of the MM it is also necessary to complete the legislative and regulatory framework associated with the restructuring of the Armed Forces; in this context the urgent approval of some regulations associated with the new organizational framework must also be included, such as the new Arsenal Regulations, the new Administration Regulations, the Regulation on infrastructural works. Finally, there is the need to redevelop the technical-logistic support structures that influence the effectiveness and efficiency of the naval and air forces, with particular reference to the Arsenals (Taranto/Brindisi, La Spezia, Augusta) and the main naval bases.
The ordering by macro functions, while it can satisfy some command needs and above all operational ones, sometimes leaves coordination problems unsolved due to the dispersion of tasks and the related responsible bodies which, however, can find the right place in multipurpose structures.
The felt need to continue the restructuring process reveals the criticality of the moment that this Armed Force is going through like the others.
To achieve this, it is necessary to have an impact, in particular, on the territorial structures of Sardinia, in which the competences of the Autonomous Maritime Military Command are transferred from the headquarters of La Maddalena to that of Cagliari; of Sicily, in which similar skills were transferred from the Messina office to that of Augusta.
Possible evolutionary lines for the Navy The provisions of the MM contained in the aforementioned Legislative Decree no. 214/2000 are characterized by the need to achieve increasingly effective operation of the military instrument in a short time and by the need to adopt solutions with a broader cost-effectiveness ratio, concentrating military personnel in certain operationally strategic geographical areas. In this sense, the organizational and operational plan of the FA provides for the presence of a territorial command, a suitable air-naval base, a production arsenal, depots and warehouses for ammunition, fuel and food supplies only in the areas - air-naval hubs - of Taranto, La Spezia and Augusta, which meet the strategic and security criteria underlying the reform.
Lastly, it is appropriate to delve deeper into the reconfiguration of the Air Regions to which tasks of a transversal nature can be attributed, i.e. in matters of common interest that go beyond the specific sphere of attributions of the individual High Commanders. ____________ Projects to concentrate operational resources, peripheral logistical structures, as well as institutes and schools of the Armed Forces must fall within this context. How studies should be continued on the various out-sourcing hypotheses in order to allocate the AM staff, currently dedicated to those sectors, to other activities more closely in line with the FA's mission.

The second consideration, also positive, is that the line of command found its correct definition with the law on the reform of top management of February 1997 and with its implementation regulation of October 1999.

Summary of the possible Evolutionary Lines on the staff The staff problems are numerous and have been addressed mainly in terms of the functionality of the instrument, sometimes neglecting the human aspect which however affects the functionality as it can cause disorientation and demotivation.
In particular, we refer to: 1. the reduction of transition times for the end of military service; 2. looking for the most valid ways to guarantee volunteers entry into the world of work; 3. the economic measures necessary to encourage the exodus of redundant staff and to improve emoluments for those who remain; 4. the restoration of command periods as an indispensable test bed for advancements; 5. the composition of the advancement commissions to guarantee the impartiality of the judgment; 6. to respect, without exceptions, the age limits for each grade; 7. the impetus to be given to the new law on military representation; 8. the redundancy of the bodies responsible for dealing with personnel in general and the volunteer recruitment system.
The current situation may also lead to discontinuities in the coordination activities between surveillance in the high seas and the territorial sea, often resolved with ad hoc coordination formulas but not always effective.
The cessation of this form of supply, which ensures more than 10% of the entire force of the Force, would produce - in the absence of adequate corrective measures - a significant decline in the operational capacity of the Institution, in the face of a constantly expanding needs framework.
There are therefore some aspects that need to be taken into account and which constitute one of the fundamental objectives of the next govemment activity.
• the surveillance and protection of maritime air spaces located outside territorial waters is an activity carried out by the Navy by virtue of both legal reserves and intemational treaties; • a multiplicity of institutional actors operate on the territorial sea with defined tasks for the performance of PS/PG activities (as a continuation of the repression of criminal activities carried out or in any case connected with the national territory) in addition to the Port Authority Corps, which carries out more specifically maritime tasks (control of merchant traffic, application of the navigation code) as well as rescue and fight against pollution at sea.

PART VI - TRAINING


239 This conceptual renewal - which, during 2001, among other things led the Armed Forces to start, among NonCommissioned Officers and Volunteers, approximately 22,000 units on courses in the national territory and approximately 2,200 units on courses/intemships/training on the jobs abroad - is being further refined, through: - the definitive adoption, in all educational institutions, of teaching by objectives, understood as the dynamic and harmonious development of the training process based on the sequential phases of needs analysis, planning, execution and verification of results;
Nor can the organizational effort made by the General Staff to guarantee the efficiency, to the limit of "survival", of the Armed Forces engaged today in dealing with employment situations which are solely for maintaining peace, be overlooked. Therefore, if instead of these, a task of a higher level of conflict were to be faced, emergency financial measures should be taken.
In fact, it is one thing to support the operation of 10,000 men in peace-keeping tasks (not even peace-enforcing) by having men and resources corresponding to 30,000 – 40,000 volunteers from the three Armed Forces Today more than yesterday, in fact, the military professional is required to have knowledge and skills that extend in every direction, necessary to allow him to face the weight of his responsibilities in the best possible way and with serene awareness in the various and complex situations in which he may find himself act, in different operating environments, in Italy and abroad.
In this context, training activities aimed at the so-called "intemationalisation" of executives have taken on particular importance to date, as they are specifically aimed at preparing Officers, Non-Commissioned Officers and Volunteers for deployment abroad or in any case in joint/multinational contexts.
Possible evolutionary lines The analytical examination of the current situation and the indication of the modemization programs necessary to guarantee the efficiency of the Armed Forces, together with the considerations formulated from time to time, clearly highlight the state of absolute necessity in which they find themselves especially the Army, Navy and Air Force. The situation can, however, be defined as satisfactory for the Carabinieri.
Furthermore, it is necessary, particularly for Officers and Non-Commissioned Officers of all the Armed Forces, to continue to focus in the future and with growing determination on deepening their historical, political, economic, legal and scientific preparation, in addition to the specific military character. on which the mass of availability has converged. It would be quite another to face greater commitments with weapon systems that are sometimes outdated, limited availability of training and war ammunition, vehicles whose inefficiencies fluctuate between 30% and 50%, limited availability of spare parts stocks and bureaucratic obstacles which limit any effort of good will aimed at making interventions and the possibility of deploying forces timely. It must also be recognized that the funding provided for missions meets the needs of personnel, but not those of materials. ____________ A similar commitment is required for the refinement of the training techniques of the volunteers who must not lose the level of efficiency achieved with the 3rd level and, above all, after the peace-keeping operations, they must revive the combat training, often weakened by checkpoint culture. Therefore, the periods between commitments in the theater of operations can and must be better calibrated both by virtue of the introduction of an increasingly higher number of volunteers into the departments and in consideration of the time necessary for reconditioning the personnel.
- the search for self-training, an area always "in progress", as a tool to improve the individual's knowledge in the technical-professional sector of his/her competence.
- the provision of greater flexibility to the teaching activity, to achieve training objectives that are always concrete and truly sustainable, through eminently practical teaching and training; - the continuous verification of the Course programmes, which must be increasingly characterized by brevity and intensity, with the aim of keeping the attendees away from the employment departments for as little time as possible;
Publications Center for Peace Studies www.studiperlapace.it For the troops, the transition phase from military service to professionalism requires careful management of the conscripts, who in their 10 months of service must carry out intensive training both to achieve the operational efficiency necessary to carry out "presence and surveillance" tasks, and so as not to feel like "second-class soldiers".
To conclude, it can be admitted that the modemization of the logistics chain has been achieved in its fundamental structures. Final adjustments are expected soon. But if the modemization plans requested by the General Staff are not implemented, we risk having created a

240 of a suitable budget for the year can at most reduce that for modemisation, without prejudice, of course, to the needs for technical and technological innovation.
Sporting activity in the Armed Forces Possible lines of evolution From an examination of the results, the preponderance of long-standing military personnel among the top ranked is evident. On the other hand, continuous commitment is natural, necessary and indispensable to achieve results "in the medal zone". Therefore Carabinieri, Financiers, State Police and Forestry Police agents are always at the top of the rankings of athletes in uniform.
Therefore the summary of what has been said can be expressed by the recognition of an absolute priority to be attributed to the materials sector, an unavoidable condition for conferring the necessary operational capacity to the Armed Forces and a preferential vehicle for achieving the attribution of superior efficiency categories also within NATO. .
Furthermore, two incremental factors in the fall in efficiency of the military structure must be kept in mind: - The first is that deriving from the intense wear and tear imposed on the vehicles by use in operations of a higher intensity than that of normal training activity in the homeland; - The second is the one connected to the summation which is determined when the inefficiencies or the rate of attrition are not immediately remedied or compensated for. The limit, in fact, translates into "not convenient repairs" and therefore into the need to purchase a new vehicle.
The transition to the professional Armed Forces will over time offer a greater possibility of results for the Army, Navy and Air Force, but at the same time, precisely due to the significant reduction of the same, it will be necessary to plan the preparation for competitive activity with a joint vision. Consequently, the Sports Centers will have to acquire the "multiweapon" characteristic by bringing together athletes who practice the same sporting discipline regardless of the Armed Force they belong to.
Military Museums Possible evolutionary lines With the reduction of military personnel due to the professionalization of the Armed Forces, the entire sector is currently being studied by the Defense General Staff to seek a solution that allows the publicization and full maintenance of the activity museum, both from the point of view of conservation of the assets contained therein, and to guarantee attendance by the public.
The possibility of establishing a single military museum in Rome is also being studied, following the example of what has been achieved in Paris, London and Brussels. The initiative must be included in the historical-reminiscent path advocated This is why "maintenance" and "operation" have merged into a single budget item. As well as the guarantee Publications Center for Peace Studies www.studiperlapace.it is also considered appropriate to draw attention to the phenomenon of "athletes" in uniform, who only carry out sporting activities without setting foot in the barracks or without any operational training activity.
A normalization effort in this sector could successfully combine sporting ethics with military ethics. ____________ Military joumalism Possible evolutionary lines At present, the problem of the lack of professionalism in the Italian Armed Forces could be addressed: - with immediate effect: by starting the qualification of personnel through attendance of the specific courses provided for by the aforementioned Implementation Regulation. Until now, personnel training is carried out according to the availability and will of each individual Armed Force with the result that courses of recognized value (organised within NATO or by organizations such as RAI) are not always carried out by personnel who would have need; - in the medium term: by inserting a "master's" into the programs of the Application Schools for the qualification of effective officers recognized as most suitable for public relations; - in a medium-term perspective: carefully evaluating the possibility of establishing a specific role for public information officials.
